pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = rowBase + x;\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch the second buffer with the same distribution as the first one so that\n// its pages are placed on the NUMA node of the thread that will write them.\nvoid touchGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                grid[rowBase + x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.\n// Contains orphaned OpenMP worksharing directives: it is meant to be called\n// from within a parallel region so that the region can be reused across\n// iterations (no repeated fork/join).\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    if (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const Real* __restrict in = input + rowBase;\n                Real* __restrict out = output + rowBase;\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const Real center = in[x];\n                    const Real left = in[x - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[x + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[x - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[x + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[x - slice];\n                    const Real top = in[x + slice];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[x] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Copy boundary values (disjoint from the interior points written above,\n    // hence the nowait on the loop before this one). The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Real* grid1 = allocateGrid(gridSize);\n    Real* grid2 = allocateGrid(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations: the worksharing loops inside\n    // stencilIteration bind to it, so threads are forked only once.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid2 : grid1;\n            stencilIteration(in, out, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1300,"cache_read_input_tokens":17898,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1300},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a44bf210-05c5-4bf5-a94a-b694fded6946","uuid":"d4f5d037-893f-42f2-b8cb-622e509f799d","timestamp":"2026-09-03T15:59:29.165Z","request_id":"req_011CegnJZtvpLaMt3b4QBJPB"}

pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = rowBase + x;\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch the second buffer with the same distribution as the first one so that\n// its pages are placed on the NUMA node of the thread that will write them.\nvoid touchGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                grid[rowBase + x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.\n// Contains orphaned OpenMP worksharing directives: it is meant to be called\n// from within a parallel region so that the region can be reused across\n// iterations (no repeated fork/join).\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    if (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const Real* __restrict in = input + rowBase;\n                Real* __restrict out = output + rowBase;\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const Real center = in[x];\n                    const Real left = in[x - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[x + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[x - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[x + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[x - slice];\n                    const Real top = in[x + slice];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[x] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Copy boundary values (disjoint from the interior points written above,\n    // hence the nowait on the loop before this one). The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation\nvoid stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, \n                      std::vector<Real>& output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                \n                const Real center = input[idx];\n                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];\n                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];\n                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];\n                \n                // Simple averaging stencil\n                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Copy boundary values\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {\n                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                    output[idx] = input[idx];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n    \n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n    for (const auto& val : grid) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n    \n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n    \n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n    \n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);\n    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);\n    \n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    \n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n        if (iter % 2 == 0) {\n            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n        } else {\n            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(finalGrid, \"Grid\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","userModified":false}}

cores still use\n// all NUMA nodes) and only falling back to SMT siblings when oversubscribed.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace placement {\n\n// CPUs we may run on, ordered physical-core-first (\"levels\" of SMT siblings).\n// coreCount is the number of entries belonging to the first level.\nstruct CpuOrder {\n    std::vector<int> \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, file);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return -1;\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) value = order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n            it->cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!haveTopology) {\n        // No topology information: use the plain CPU list.\n        order.cpus = allowed;\n        order.coreCount = allowed.size();\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [](const Core& a, const Core& b) { return a.key < b.key; });\n    size_t maxSiblings = 0;\n    for (const c.cpus.size()) order.cpus.push_back(c.cpus[level]);\n        }\n        if (level == 0) order.coreCount = order.cpus.size();\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\nstatic void pinThread(const CpuOrder& order, const int threadId, const int numThreads) {\n    if (order.cpus.empty() || numThreads <= 0) return;\n    // Stretch the threads evenly over the physical cores; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads. Both steps are skipped if the\n// user expressed a preference through the environment.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    const CpuOrder order = buildCpuOrder();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && order.coreCount > 0) {\n        // Default to one thread per physical core: SMT siblings share the\n        // core's load/store resources and do not add memory bandwidth.\n        omp_set_num_threads((int)std::min(order.coreCount, (size_t)omp_get_max_threads()));\n    }\n\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    { pinThread(order, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads()); }\n#endif\n}\n\n}  // namespace placement"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3689,"cache_read_input_tokens":34805,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3689},"output_tokens":17,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a44bf210-05c5-4bf5-a94a-b694fded6946","uuid":"b4886bce-abcc-473e-8323-b05e87b7fca9","timestamp":"2026-09-03T16:03:04.419Z","request_id":"req_011CegneQKXKaewquu4JNi58"}

not request a placement policy via OMP_PROC_BIND, pin the\n// threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, stretched evenly over the\n// cores we are allowed to run on (so that fewer threads than cores still use\n// all NUMA nodes) and only falling back to SMT siblings when oversubscribed.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace placement {\n\n// CPUs we may run on, ordered physical-core-first (\"levels\" of SMT siblings).\n// coreCount is the number of entries belonging to the first level.\nstruct CpuOrder {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    size_t coreCount = 0;\n};\n\n#ifdef __linux__\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* file) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, file);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return -1;\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) value = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\nstatic CpuOrder buildCpuOrder() {\n    CpuOrder order;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return order;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) allowed.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (allowed.empty()) return order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n allowed.size();\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [](const Core& a, const Core& b) { return a.key < b.key; });\n    size_t maxSiblings = 0;\n    for (const Core& c : cores) maxSiblings = std::max(maxSiblings, c.cpus.size());\n\n    for (size_t level = 0; level < maxSiblings; ++level) {\n        for (const Core& c : cores) {\n            if (level < c.cpus.size()) order.cpus.push_back(c.cpus[level]);\n        }\n        if (level == 0) order.coreCount = order.cpus.size();\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\nstatic void pinThread(const CpuOrder& order, const int threadId, const int numThreads) {\n    if (order.cpus.empty() || numThreads <= 0) return;\n    // Stretch the threads evenly over the physical cores; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads. Both steps are skipped if the\n// user expressed a preference through the environment.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    const CpuOrder order = buildCpuOrder();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && order.coreCount > 0) {\n        // Default to one thread per physical core: SMT siblings share the\n        // core's load/store resources and do not add memory bandwidth.\n        omp_set_num_threads((int)std::min(order.coreCount, (size_t)omp_get_max_threads()));\n    }\n\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    { pinThread(order, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads()); pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = rowBase + x;\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch the second buffer with the same distribution as the first one so that\n// its pages are placed on the NUMA node of the thread that will write them.\nvoid touchGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                grid[rowBase + x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.\n// Contains orphaned OpenMP worksharing directives: it is meant to be called\n// from within a parallel region so that the region can be reused across\n// iterations (no repeated fork/join).\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    if (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const Real center = in[x];\n                    const Real left = in[x - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[x + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[x - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[x + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[x - slice];\n                    const Real top = in[x + slice];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[x] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Copy boundary values (disjoint from the interior points written above,\n    // hence the nowait on the loop before this one). The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Thread placement\n//\n// This kernel is memory-bandwidth bound, so its performance depends entirely on\n// threads staying on the core whose NUMA node holds the data they first touched.\n// If the user did not request a placement policy via OMP_PROC_BIND, pin the\n// threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, stretched evenly over the\n// cores we are allowed to run on (so that fewer threads than cores still use\n// all NUMA nodes) and only falling back to SMT siblings when oversubscribed.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace placement {\n\n// CPUs we may run on, ordered physical-core-first (\"levels\" of SMT siblings).\n// coreCount is the number of entries belonging to the first level.\nstruct CpuOrder {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    size_t coreCount = 0;\n};\n\n#ifdef __linux__\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* file) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, file);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return -1;\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) value = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\nstatic CpuOrder buildCpuOrder() {\n    CpuOrder order;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return order;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) allowed.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (allowed.empty()) return order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n            it->cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!haveTopology) {\n        // No topology information: use the plain CPU list.\n        order.cpus = allowed;\n        order.coreCount = allowed.size();\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [](const Core& a, const Core& b) { return a.key < b.key; });\n    size_t maxSiblings = 0;\n    for (const Core& c : cores) maxSiblings = std::max(maxSiblings, c.cpus.size());\n\n    for (size_t level = 0; level < maxSiblings; ++level) {\n        for (const Core& c : cores) {\n            if (level < c.cpus.size()) order.cpus.push_back(c.cpus[level]);\n        }\n        if (level == 0) order.coreCount = order.cpus.size();\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\nstatic void pinThread(const CpuOrder& order, const int threadId, const int numThreads) {\n    if (order.cpus.empty() || numThreads <= 0) return;\n    // Stretch the threads evenly over the physical cores; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads. Both steps are skipped if the\n// user expressed a preference through the environment.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    const CpuOrder order = buildCpuOrder();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && order.coreCount > 0) {\n        // Default to one thread per physical core: SMT siblings share the\n        // core's load/store resources and do not add memory bandwidth.\n        omp_set_num_threads((int)std::min(order.coreCount, (size_t)omp_get_max_threads()));\n    }\n\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    { pinThread(order, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads()); }\n#endif\n}\n\n}  // namespace placement\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Aligned allocation of an untouched buffer; pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = rowBase + x;\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch the second buffer with the same distribution as the first one so that\n// its pages are placed on the NUMA node of the thread that will write them.\nvoid touchGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                grid[rowBase + x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.\n// Contains orphaned OpenMP worksharing directives: it is meant to be called\n// from within a parallel region so that the region can be reused across\n// iterations (no repeated fork/join).\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    if (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const Real* __restrict in = input + rowBase;\n                Real* __restrict out = output + rowBase;\n#pragma omp simd\n                for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n                    const Real center = in[x];\n                    const Real left = in[x - 1];\n                    const Real right = in[x + 1];\n                    const Real front = in[x - nx];\n                    const Real back = in[x + nx];\n                    const Real bottom = in[x - slice];\n                    const Real top = in[x + slice];\n\n                    // Simple averaging stencil\n                    out[x] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Copy boundary values (disjoint from the interior points written above,\n    // hence the nowait on the loop before this one). The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified\n    int iterations = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-x\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-y\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-z\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;\n    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;\n\n    printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n\n    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    // Allocate grids (double buffering)\n    Real* grid1 = allocateGrid(gridSize);\n    Real* grid2 = allocateGrid(gridSize);\n\n    // Initialize\n    printf(\"Initializing grid...\\n\");\n    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);\n    touchGrid(grid2, nx, ny, nz);\n\n    // Run stencil iterations\n    printf(\"Running stencil computation...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations: the worksharing loops inside\n    // stencilIteration bind to it, so threads are forked only once.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {\n            const Real* in = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n            Real* out = (iter % 2 == 0) ? grid2 : grid1;\n            stencilIteration(in, out, nx, ny, nz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Calculate performance metrics\n    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;\n    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":336,"oldLines":6,"newStart":336,"newLines":9,"lines":["     if (ny == 0) ny = nx;","     if (nz == 0) nz = nx;"," ","+    // Must happen before the grids are first touched.","+    placement::configureThreads();","+","     printf(\"3D Stencil Benchmark\\n\");","     printf(\"Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\\n\", nx, ny, nz);","     printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", iterations);"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) allowed.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (allowed.empty()) return order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n            it->cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!haveTopology) {\n        // No topology information: use the plain CPU list.\n        order.cpus = allowed;\n        order.coreCount = allowed.size();\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [](const Core& a, const Core& b) { return a.key < b.key; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads. Both steps are skipped if the\n// user expressed a preference through the environment.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    const CpuOrder order = buildCpuOrder();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && order.coreCount > 0) {\n        // Default to one thread per physical core: SMT siblings share the\n        // core's load/store resources and do not add memory bandwidth.\n        omp_set_num_threads((int)std::min(order.coreCount, (size_t)omp_get_max_threads()));\n    }\n\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    { pinThread(order, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads()); pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing Real = double;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Thread placement\n//\n// This kernel is memory-bandwidth bound, so its performance depends entirely on\n// threads staying on the core whose NUMA node holds the data they first touched.\n// If the user did not request a placement policy via OMP_PROC_BIND, pin the\n// threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, stretched evenly over the\n// cores we are allowed to run on (so that fewer threads than cores still use\n// all NUMA nodes) and only falling back to SMT siblings when oversubscribed.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace placement {\n\n// CPUs we may run on, ordered physical-core-first (\"levels\" of SMT siblings).\n// coreCount is the number of entries belonging to the first level.\nstruct CpuOrder {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    size_t coreCount = 0;\n};\n\n#ifdef __linux__\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* file) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, file);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return -1;\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) value = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\nstatic CpuOrder buildCpuOrder() {\n    CpuOrder order;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return order;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) allowed.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (allowed.empty()) return order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n            it->cpus.push_back(cpu);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!haveTopology) {\n        // No topology information: use the plain CPU list.\n        order.cpus = allowed;\n        order.coreCount = allowed.size();\n        return order;\n    }\n\n    std::sort(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [](const Core& a, const Core& b) { return a.key < b.key; });\n    size_t maxSiblings = 0;\n    for (const Core& c : cores) maxSiblings = std::max(maxSiblings, c.cpus.size());\n\n    for (size_t level = 0; level < maxSiblings; ++level) {\n        for (const Core& c : cores) {\n            if (level < c.cpus.size()) order.cpus.push_back(c.cpus[level]);\n        }\n        if (level == 0) order.coreCount = order.cpus.size();\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\nstatic void pinThread(const CpuOrder& order, const int threadId, const int numThreads) {\n    if (order.cpus.empty() || numThreads <= 0) return;\n    // Stretch the threads evenly over the physical cores; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads. Both steps are skipped if the\n// user expressed a preference through the environment.\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    const CpuOrder order = buildCpuOrder();\n\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") == nullptr && order.coreCount > 0) {\n        // Default to one thread per physical core: SMT siblings share the\n        // core's load/store resources and do not add memory bandwidth.\n        omp_set_num_threads((int)std::min(order.coreCount, (size_t)omp_get_max_threads()));\n    }\n\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    { pinThread(order, omp_get_thread_num(), omp_get_num_threads()); }\n#endif\n}\n\n}  // namespace placement\n\n// 3D index calculation\ninline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {\n    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n}\n\n// Aligned allocation of an untouched buffer; pages are faulted in later by the\n// threads that own them (first touch), which keeps the data NUMA-local.\nstatic Real* allocateGrid(const size_t n) {\n    constexpr size_t alignment = 64;\n    const size_t bytes = ((n * sizeof(Real) + alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;\n    void* p = std::aligned_alloc(alignment, bytes);\n    if (p == nullptr) {\n        printf(\"Allocation of %zu bytes failed\\n\", bytes);\n        exit(1);\n    }\n    return static_cast<Real*>(p);\n}\n\n// The distribution used for first touch must match the one used by the stencil\n// loops, so both use a static schedule over the collapsed (z, y) plane rows.\nvoid initializeGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = rowBase + x;\n                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Touch the second buffer with the same distribution as the first one so that\n// its pages are placed on the NUMA node of the thread that will write them.\nvoid touchGrid(Real* __restrict grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n#pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                grid[rowBase + x] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Update one interior row [1, nx-1) with ordinary (cached) stores.\nstatic inline void stencilRow(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out,\n                              const size_t nx, const size_t slice, size_t x) {\n#pragma omp simd\n    for (; x < nx - 1; ++x) {\n        const Real center = in[x];\n        const Real left = in[x - 1];\n        const Real right = in[x + 1];\n        const Real front = in[x - nx];\n        const Real back = in[x + nx];\n        const Real bottom = in[x - slice];\n        const Real top = in[x + slice];\n\n        // Simple averaging stencil\n        out[x] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n// Same computation (same operand order, hence bit-identical results) but with\n// streaming stores, which avoid pulling the output cache lines in for writing.\n// Only worthwhile when the grids do not fit in the last level caches.\nstatic inline void stencilRowStreaming(const Real* __restrict in, Real* __restrict out,\n                                       const size_t nx, const size_t slice) {\n    constexpr size_t W = 4;  // doubles per 256-bit vector\n    size_t x = 1;\n    // Peel until the store address is vector aligned.\n    while (x < nx - 1 && (reinterpret_cast<uintptr_t>(out + x) % (W * sizeof(Real))) != 0) {\n        out[x] = (in[x] + in[x - 1] + in[x + 1] + in[x - nx] + in[x + nx] + in[x - slice] + in[x + slice]) / 7.0;\n        ++x;\n    }\n    const __m256d seven = _mm256_set1_pd(7.0);\n    for (; x + W <= nx - 1; x += W) {\n        __m256d s = _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x);\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x - 1));\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x + 1));\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x - nx));\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x + nx));\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x - slice));\n        s = _mm256_add_pd(s, _mm256_loadu_pd(in + x + slice));\n        _mm256_stream_pd(out + x, _mm256_div_pd(s, seven));\n    }\n    // Remainder\n    stencilRow(in, out, nx, slice, x);\n}\n#endif\n\n// 7-point stencil computation.\n// Contains orphaned OpenMP worksharing directives: it is meant to be called\n// from within a parallel region so that the region can be reused across\n// iterations (no repeated fork/join).\nvoid stencilIteration(const Real* __restrict input,\n                      Real* __restrict output,\n                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,\n                      const bool streamingStores) {\n    const size_t slice = nx * ny;\n\n    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)\n    if (nx > 2 && ny > 2 && nz > 2) {\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static) nowait\n        for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {\n            for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {\n                const size_t rowBase = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                const Real* __restrict in = input + rowBase;\n                Real* __restrict out = output + rowBase;\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n                if (streamingStores) {\n                    stencilRowStreaming(in, out, nx, slice);\n                    continue;\n                }\n#else\n                (void)streamingStores;\n#endif\n                stencilRow(in, out, nx, slice, 1);\n            }\n        }\n#if defined(__AVX__)\n        // Streaming stores are weakly ordered; make them visible before the\n        // barrier at the end of the boundary loop below.\n        if (streamingStores) _mm_sfence();\n#endif\n    }\n\n    // Copy boundary values (disjoint from the interior points written above,\n    // hence the nowait on the loop before this one). The implicit barrier at\n    // the end of this loop separates the iterations.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        if (z == 0 || z == nz - 1) {\n            // Full plane\n            const size_t base = z * slice;\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < slice; ++i) {\n                output[base + i] = input[base + i];\n            }\n        } else {\n            // y boundary rows\n            for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; y += (ny > 1 ? ny - 1 : 1)) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                    output[base + x] = input[base + x];\n                }\n            }\n            // x boundary columns of the interior rows\n            for (size_t y = 1; y + 1 < ny; ++y) {\n                const size_t base = idx3(0, y, z, nx, ny);\n                output[base] = input[base];\n                output[base + nx - 1] = input[base + nx - 1];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const Real* __restrict grid, const size_t gridSize) {\n    // Simple sanity checks\n\n    // 1. No NaN or Inf values\n    bool bad = false;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(|| : bad)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        const Real val = grid[i];\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            bad = true;\n        }\n    }\n    if (bad) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)\n    Real minVal = grid[0];\n    Real maxVal = grid[0];\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < gridSize; ++i) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, grid[i]);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, grid[i]);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded\n    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly\n    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t nx = 128;\n    size_t ny = 0;  // Million cell updates per second\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\n\", mcups);\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    const Real* finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;\n    if (printResults) {\n        const std::vector<Real> finalVec(finalGrid, finalGrid + gridSize);\n        print_results(finalVec, \"Grid\");\n    }\n\n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, gridSize);\n\n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            std::free(grid1);\n            std::free(grid2);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    std::free(grid1);\n    std::free(grid2);\n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":4,"oldLines":13,"newStart":4,"newLines":19,"lines":[" #include <cstdio>"," #include <cstdlib>"," #include <cstring>","+#include <cstdint>"," #include <memory>"," #include <vector>"," "," #include <omp.h>"," ","+#if defined(__AVX__)","+#include <immintrin.h>","+#endif","+"," #ifdef __linux__"," #include <sched.h>","+#include <unistd.h>"," #endif"," "," #include \"../common/results_output.hpp\""]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

placement\n//\n// This kernel is memory-bandwidth bound, so its performance depends entirely on\n// threads staying on the core whose NUMA node holds the data they first touched.\n// If the user did not request a placement policy via OMP_PROC_BIND, pin the\n// threads ourselves: one thread per physical core, stretched evenly over the\n// cores we are allowed to run on (so that fewer threads than cores still use\n// all NUMA nodes) and only falling back to SMT siblings when oversubscribed.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace placement {\n\n// CPUs we may run on, ordered physical-core-first (\"levels\" of SMT siblings).\n// coreCount is the number of entries belonging to the first level.\nstruct CpuOrder {\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    size_t coreCount = 0;\n};\n\n#ifdef __linux__\nstatic int readTopologyId(const int cpu, const char* file) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, file);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (f == nullptr) return -1;\n    int value = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%d\", &value) != 1) value = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return value;\n}\n\nstatic CpuOrder buildCpuOrder() {\n    CpuOrder order;\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask) != 0) return order;\n\n    std::vector<int> allowed;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &mask)) allowed.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (allowed.empty()) return order;\n\n    // Group the allowed CPUs by physical core, keeping package/core order.\n    struct Core {\n        long long key;\n        std::vector<int> cpus;\n    };\n    std::vector<Core> cores;\n    bool haveTopology = true;\n    for (const int cpu : allowed) {\n        const int pkg = readTopologyId(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const int core = readTopologyId(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) {\n            haveTopology = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        const long long key = (long long)pkg * 1000000 + core;\n        auto it = std::find_if(cores.begin(), cores.end(), [key](const Core& c) { return c.key == key; });\n        if (it == cores.end()) {\n            cores.push_back(Core{key, {cpu}});\n        } else {\n c.cpus.size()) order.cpus.push_back(c.cpus[level]);\n        }\n        if (level == 0) order.coreCount = order.cpus.size();\n    }\n    return order;\n}\n\nstatic void pinThread(const CpuOrder& order, const int threadId, const int numThreads) {\n    if (order.cpus.empty() || numThreads <= 0) return;\n    // Stretch the threads evenly over the physical cores; use the full list\n    // (including SMT siblings) only when there are more threads than cores.\n    const size_t pool = ((size_t)numThreads <= order.coreCount && order.coreCount > 0) ? order.coreCount : order.cpus.size();\n    const size_t slot = ((size_t)threadId * pool) / (size_t)numThreads;\n    const int cpu = order.cpus[std::min(slot, pool - 1)];\n\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n    sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask);\n}\n#endif  // __linux__\n\n// Choose the thread count and pin the threads.
